# Agent 2 — Tenders & Procurement — Findings 2026-08-27

(Method: shared research harness — one agent per procurement portal, adversarial per-claim verification, completeness gate, DETERMINISTIC table render. 15 procurement portal(s) targeted, 15 returned signals, 263 verified signals. Confidence capped at Medium per single-agent playbook. Table rows ordered by Printec value (size x win), biddable first.)
(Run note: the first harness pass lost 25 of 47 agents to a session limit; 8 procurement portals died at the verify stage AFTER their research checkpoints had been written to disk. Those 150 researched signals were backed up (parts/2026-08-27_run1-orphaned-backup) and re-verified in 17 batches of <=11 signals each (the documented fix for the 32k output-token cap), then merged by script (weekly-intelligence/scripts/merge_harness_runs.py) — the table is machine-rendered, never LLM-written. The narrate step also died on the session limit and the prose was written by the parent)

## What changed since last run

**The single biggest change is a loss.** Banka Slovenije's banknote-sorter maintenance — the item this brief flagged on 01/08/2026 as the most directly biddable cash-automation contest in the pipeline — **was awarded on 21/08/2026 to Giesecke+Devrient Currency Technology GmbH (Munich) at EUR 1,890,000 excl. VAT on a single bid**, running six years to 31/08/2032 (open procedure JN005737/2026-EUe16/01 = TED 495378-2026; scope is the BPS M7 MEvo-12SBN sorter plus the standby MEvo-10LSBN, both with Nota Tracc and LVM modules). The mechanics matter more than the result: a **first attempt at the identical contract was cancelled on 03/07/2026** (JN003263/2026 = TED 287173-2026) after the same sole bidder quoted **exactly the same EUR 1,890,000**, and the re-tender then accepted that price — which is **+96% on the previous six-year contract** the same buyer awarded at EUR 963,000. A central bank re-ran a procurement and got one bid at an unchanged, doubled price. That is a captured account with no competitive tension, and it is the clearest evidence this run that OEM-locked sorter maintenance is where the margin sits.

**Ukraine produced the one genuinely urgent item, and Printec is not on the list.** PrivatBank's own portal published **PB-2026-08-20-3072069, 'Сортувальник банкнот 2-кишеньковий 2 тур (2 етап)' (2-pocket banknote sorter, stage 2), EUR 1,538,156.59 including VAT, bids closing 28/08/2026 08:00 UTC with the electronic auction the same morning**. Unusually, the portal publishes the stage-1 qualified shortlist even though it never publishes winners, so the four firms admitted to stage 2 are on the record: **Glory Global Solutions (International) Limited, TOV «Банківські технології» (EDRPOU 33417694), TOV «ДІІП 2000» (EDRPOU 40754185) and TOV «Інноваційні банківські системи» (EDRPOU 36184511)**. Printec is not among them — this is a named four-way competitive set for a EUR 1.5m cash-automation contract closing tomorrow.

**The 800-ATM recycler programme has stalled, not moved.** PB-2026-03-17-3068329 'Ресайклінговий банкомат (1 етап)' has now sat at portal status `active.stage2.waiting` for five months: stage 1 (technical qualification) is finished, PrivatBank has not issued the stage-2 commercial round, and no shortlisted-bidder node has been published. A full-text Prozorro sweep confirms **no 2026 recycler-hardware award exists** anywhere in the public record. Printec's incumbency was re-verified on the primary Prozorro record UA-2025-01-17-010779-a (17/01/2025, Art. 35 p.3 urgent-need procurement without the e-system) — **UAH 838,639,173.73 incl. VAT (~EUR 16.1m) for 800× NCR SelfServ 2062 plus 340× NCR SelfServ 2064, i.e. 1,140 recycling ATMs, delivered by 30/11/2025** — though note one cross-check found that the linked portal tender PB-2024-05-20-3055047 publishes no supplier name of its own, so treat the 1,140-unit attribution as resting on the Prozorro award record rather than on the bank's portal. The 2026 programme is a **separate 800 machines**, per board member Dmytro Musiienko on 21/05/2026. The window to defend this is the stage-2 issuance, and it is still open.

**Geldservice Austria's EUR 9.1m recycler framework is still not awarded** — re-verified twice this run, from TED and from the Austrian USP portal. TED holds exactly one GSA notice in the whole of 2026 (305531-2026, published 05/05/2026), the participation deadline was 08/06/2026, and roughly eleven weeks later there is no contract-award notice. Contract start is recorded as 02/11/2026 on an open-ended term (the notice states 999 months; the EUR 9.1m is a five-year estimate covering devices *and* maintenance), with award points split across evaluated total price 245, banknote recycling 100 and coin recycling 74. It remains the largest single cash-automation contest in the footprint and it is still live.

**Greece opened an XL pre-tender rather than a tender.** ELTA's procurement, run by EESYP/Growthfund under the Government Committee on Strategic Contracts (session 27/07/2026), issued an amended **preliminary market consultation for a 'Retail & Delivery Transformation Initiative — Integrated Retail Systems & Delivery Control Tower and Track & Trace', budget EUR 6,000,000 excl. VAT, ELTA-financed, comments due 10/09/2026 through ESIDIS** (ref 2026DIAB33462). A market consultation is the stage at which specifications are still movable, which makes it worth more than most open tenders in this file.

**Two more open items are worth naming now.** Prague's city ICT operator republished its **payment-gateway procurement (CZK 250,000,000, TED 566770-2026) with the deadline moved to 31/08/2026** — the previous read of it as closed was wrong; it is open and biddable. And the **Bulgarian National Bank's EUR 115,480,000 euro coin-blank procurement closed 03/08/2026 with no award notice published**, delivering to both the Bulgarian Mint and Mincovňa Kremnica.

**Structurally, two negatives hardened into findings.** Hungary's Act XVIII ATM mandate was re-verified line by line against the primary decree texts and **generates zero procurement footprint** — allocation is bank-by-bank by MNB decree, so there is nothing to bid and the route is direct engagement with the nine assigned banks. Serbia's portal was swept and confirms the same shape for a different reason: commercial banks there are private and simply do not appear, which is why the Serbian ATM pipeline is central-bank and state-post only. Both are honest zeros, not coverage gaps.

**Verification also cleaned up the baseline.** Several carried items were re-dated or corrected: the Slovak Post security-support tender was **extended to 08/09/2026** (EUR 1,745,311.25) and NBS Slovakia's LAN expansion **extended to 31/08/2026**; Cyprus's Department of Insolvency EUR 5,652,000 integrated-system tender is now on its **sixth publication, deadline 15/09/2026**; the Romanian Post CIT tender is open to 21/09/2026; and Croatian Post's technical-protection tender (EUR 1,150,000) closed 17/08/2026 with no award yet.

## Top opportunities (ranked)

1. **PrivatBank (Ukraine)** — the 800-machine 2026 recycler refresh, stalled at stage-2 issuance on the bank's own portal. Printec holds the 1,140-unit predecessor; the entire contest is the stage-2 round that has not yet been called. Engage now, before it is issued (XL).
2. **ELTA / Hellenic Post (Greece)** — EUR 6m Retail & Delivery Transformation market consultation open to 10/09/2026, plus the standing all-bank fee-free ATM concept across the post network, where Printec's Cashflex/KEA is the natural vehicle. Specifications are still movable (XL).
3. **Geldservice Austria** — EUR 9.1m combined deposit/withdrawal recycler framework, open-ended term, delivery into Slovenia as well as Austria, still unawarded eleven weeks past the participation deadline (XL).
4. **Department of Insolvency (Cyprus)** — EUR 5,652,000 integrated system plus IaaS, sixth publication, closing 15/09/2026. Repeated republication signals a market that has not yet formed around it (XL).
5. **Operátor ICT / City of Prague (Czechia)** — CZK 250,000,000 payment gateway, open to 31/08/2026, awarded on most-economically-advantageous rather than lowest price (XL).
6. **PrivatBank banknote sorters (Ukraine)** — EUR 1,538,156.59 stage-2 round closing 28/08/2026 against a named four-firm shortlist. Not winnable this week, but it prices the segment and names the competition (L).
7. **Banka Slovenije aftermath (Slovenia)** — the sorter-maintenance account is lost to Giesecke+Devrient to 2032, but the +96% single-bid price on a re-tendered contract is the argument to take to every other central bank on the same OEM lock (L, defensive intelligence).
8. **C.N. Poşta Română (Romania)** — cash-transport services tender still biddable at 27/08/2026, closing 21/09/2026 (L).
9. **National Bank of Serbia (Serbia)** — money-counting machines, ЈН 397/2026, open to 11/09/2026, plus the Glory UW-F4 maintenance round closing 28/08/2026 (M).
10. **Bank of Albania (Albania)** — three open tenders running to 09/09, 11/09 and 17/09/2026 across security-system maintenance and the data-centre refresh cycle (M).

Both decrees remain in force UNAMENDED (consolidated-text stamps re-read this run: NGM 16/2025 'Hatály: 2025.VI.6. -'; MNB 19/2025 'Hatály: 2025.VI.27. -'; no 2026 amendment). NGM 16/2025 §1 sets the two phases on 2022 KSH census data: (a) from 31/12/2025 in every settlement with more than 1,000 inhabitants, (b) from 31/12/2026 in every settlement with more than 500 inhabitants. MNB 19/2025 Annex 1 ('A pénzforgalmi szolgáltatók telepítéssel érintett településenkénti meghatározása' — the determination, settlement by settlement, of which payment service provider is obliged) lists 1,039 named settlements grouped into 80 SETTLEMENT PACKAGES (településcsomag), each package assigned to ONE obligated payment service provider. Package split re-counted directly from Annex 1 this run and reproduced exactly: OTP Bank Nyrt. 33, MBH Bank Nyrt. 15, K&H Bank Zrt. 9, ERSTE BANK HUNGARY Zrt. 8, Raiffeisen Bank Zrt. 5, CIB Bank Zrt. 4, UniCredit Bank Hungary Zrt. 4, GRÁNIT Bank Nyrt. 1, MagNet Bank Zrt. 1 — nine institutions, 80 packages, 1,039 settlements. THE DECREE PUBLISHES NO PER-BANK ATM COUNT: Annex 1 has only three columns (package number, settlement name, obligated provider), so the per-bank ATM numbers struck from the 01/08 file are still NOT restated — no such column exists. ALLOCATION FORMULA (MNB 19/2025 §1(1)(a), added this run): the burden is split on 2024 data weighted 20% payment-service fee income, 30% number of issued debit cards with a cash-withdrawal function at 31/12/2024, 10% number of consumer payment accounts, 15% number of non-zero-balance retail HUF sight deposits, 25% balance-sheet total, group-level for banking groups; §1(1)(b) then weights each affected settlement by district gross average earnings, population, unemployment rate and share of residents working elsewhere. §3(2)-(3) let providers swap packages or individual settlements and take over each other's obligations by contract (a hand-over of OPERATION cannot be contracted for less than 5 years), with contract deadlines 20/07/2025 for phase (a) and 28/02/2026 for phase (b), notified to MNB through the ERA system by 31/07/2025 and 16/03/2026 respectively; §4(3) requires MNB to review the allocation at least every three years; §4(4) obliges an incoming operator to reimburse the previous operator's documented ATM-installation costs incurred within 3 years. NGM 16/2025 §2 requires the municipality to provide a site that is easily reachable and traffic-frequented, barrier-free, properly lit, has power and internet, and where the ATM can be safely installed, serviced and cash-loaded. OTP Bank, joined by Erste Bank Hungary and Raiffeisen Bank, filed a constitutional complaint with the Alkotmánybíróság (Constitutional Court) on 10/11/2025 arguing the mandate is economically unjustified and disproportionately restricts their property and business rights; NO decision found as of 27/08/2026 — status remains unverified. | [Nemzeti Jogszabálytár / net.jogtar.hu consolidated texts of MNB decree 19/2025 (VI. 26.) Note Salt Bank, not SelfPay, is the contracting counterparty, so the commercial conversation is with a bank. |

| Slovak merchants / acquirers (Act 384/2025 Z. z. cashless-acceptance mandate, as postponed by Act 30/2026 Z. z.) | Slovakia | [STANDING — dated 2025/2026, re-checked this run] Act No. 384/2025 Z. z. on the recording of sales (o evidencii tržieb) obliges sellers to let the customer pay cashlessly on purchases ABOVE EUR 1, with sanctions for non-compliance. The commencement of that duty sits in § 19 ods. 5 of the Act, and it was POSTPONED from the originally legislated 01/03/2026 to 01/05/2026 by Act No. 30/2026 Z. z. (adopted 12/02/2026, published in the Zbierka zákonov 27/02/2026), which amended § 19 ods. 5 specifically. The law explicitly permits alternatives to a card terminal — notably a QR code scanned and paid from a mobile banking app, or any other payment method the seller chooses as suited to the point of sale — so it does NOT compel a POS terminal per merchant. The terminal-count movement carried from the baseline (70,943 to 77,711 POS terminals between 31/12/2025 and 31/05/2026, +9.5%) COULD NOT BE VERIFIED against NBS payment statistics this run and should be treated as unconfirmed. Nothing in the ÚVO Vestník relates to this mandate: it drives private merchant and acquirer purchasing, not public procurement. Working routes for Croatian procurement this run were (a) the TED Search API v3 for everything above threshold — including full-text queries, which surfaced two notices that buyer- and CPV-based sweeps missed; and (b) buyers' own procurement-plan PDFs extracted locally with pdfminer, where the HNB plan v18 dated 24/08/2026 alone yielded six cash-processing lines invisible on TED. | [Direct access re-test of eojn.hr, /procurements-public and /api/postupci with a desktop Chrome UA](https://eojn.hr/procurements-public) | 27/08/2026 | Below-threshold Croatian procurement ('jednostavna nabava', e.g. HNB's P-407/2026 mid-size banknote sorter at EUR 26,000) is structurally invisible to automated collection. | Cyprus commercial banks and eprocurement.gov.cy coverage (structural) | Cyprus | [STRUCTURAL FINDING — a documented ZERO, with one correction] Across this run no open or awarded Cyprus public-procurement notice was found for: ATMs or ATM software, ATM recyclers, self-service or branch kiosks, POS terminals or terminal management, softPOS, instant payments / ISO 20022 / RTGS, transaction-monitoring or fraud systems, HSMs or payment key management, or physical ATM security (anti-skimming / IBNS). CORRECTION: payments infrastructure is NOT a total zero — the Central Bank of Cyprus awarded Tender 06/2024, 'Administration, maintenance and support of the SWIFT system and its related infrastructure', to ALLIED ENGINEERING GROUP (CYPRUS) LTD for EUR 415,000 (TED contract-award notice 667724-2025, published 10/10/2025, restricted procedure, 1 tender, CPV 72261000, contract issue 29/09/2025). The rest of the finding is structural and stands: Cyprus's five commercial banks — Bank of Cyprus, Hellenic Bank, Alpha Bank Cyprus, Eurobank Cyprus and AstroBank — are PRIVATE companies and are not contracting authorities, so their ATM/POS/self-service buying never reaches eprocurement.gov.cy or TED. The only Cyprus BANK that is a contracting authority is the state-owned Οργανισμός Χρηματοδοτήσεως Στέγης (Housing Finance Corporation). The Central Bank of Cyprus procures OUTSIDE eprocurement.gov.cy entirely, under its own 'Directives on the Regulation of Contract Award Procedures 2021 to 2025' (a regulatory administrative act harmonised with Directive 2014/24/EU), publishing in the Official Gazette of the Republic and on centralbank.cy, and on TED only above threshold — a statement confirmed verbatim on the CBC tenders page re-fetched 27/08/2026. The nearest cash-automation demand in the public feed comes from NON-BANKS: the Electricity Authority of Cyprus (banknote counters, EUR 55,000 estimate) and the Treasury (cash-in-transit, EUR 600,000 estimate). Do NOT report Cyprus as 'no ATM/POS demand' — report it as demand that is invisible to procurement feeds. | Structural; unchanged over the forecast horizon | Medium | Unscoped | — | Reclassify Cyprus in the coverage plan from a tender-monitored market to a direct-account market, with named-account plans for Bank of Cyprus (existing Glory TCR reference), Hellenic Bank, Alpha Bank Cyprus, Eurobank Cyprus, AstroBank and JCC Payment Systems. Every award found this run, regardless of size, with the winner named. Per the playbook's competitor-scope rule a few-€m (or few-€k) local winner is a first-class competitor: these are firms bidding the contracts Printec bids. ## Verification notes
- **Corrected/contradicted by the adversarial pass (33):** CEC Bank S.A. (state-owned commercial bank, RO361897) — as public-sector card acquirer — Every contract value CONFIRMED, several buyer names CORRECTED. Re-fetched SICAP api-pub C_PUBLIC_CANotice/GetCANoticeContracts for all nine caNoticeIds on 27/08/2026: the contract-by-contract sums reproduce the researcher's figures EXACTLY (2,926,882.55 / 2,289,600.48 / 2,055,793.87 / 2,302,498.40 / 1,151,778.33 / 1,014,500 / 4,457,700 / 10,756,800 / 3,755,529.65) and every winner object is 'CEC Bank' fiscalNumber 'RO361897', so the fiscal code is confirmed too. CORRECTIONS from the SICAP notice-list records: (a) SCNA1134431's buyer is '12380248 - DIRECTIA GENERALA DE IMPOZITE SI TAXE LOCALE SECTOR 6', not Sector 1 as the researcher wrote — this is a real error and the reason this item is marked contradicted; (b) the three notices the researcher labelled only 'DGRFP' are DGRFP Iaşi (CAN1151682), DGRFP Braşov (CAN1167371) and DGRFP Ploieşti (CAN1172870) — named now; (c) CAN1156763's buyer is C.N. Poşta Română and its title is 'Servicii bancare pentru procesarea plăţilor cu cardul în mediul online', not a 'TEZAUR' product, and its contract is dated 23/10/2025 with the award notice only published 07/08/2026 — so that RON 10.76m is a 2025 contract surfaced in 2026, not new spend. Independent corroboration: TED API search winner-name="CEC BANK" AND publication-date>=20260501 returns 22 award notices, which supports the 'most-awarded acquirer' claim on a second primary source and adds the Ministry of Finance and Craiova/Arad wins listed above. CAN1172870 confirmed on TED as 564413-2026 (14/08/2026, DGRFP Ploieşti, total-value 4,457,700) and CAN1173223 as 583545-2026 (24/08/2026, Loteria Română, total-value 3,755,529.65). Values are RON as published; the notices do not state a VAT basis so none is asserted. Confidence capped at Medium.; Operátor ICT, a.s. (City of Prague ICT operator) — Value and criterion confirmed (CZK 250,000,000, CPV 66110000, buyer Operátor ICT a.s. IČO 02795281). CONTRADICTED on status: the original signal reported the tender as closed on 24/08/2026 awaiting award. Querying all Operátor ICT notices in the window returned a third notice, TED 566770-2026 published 17/08/2026, republishing the procurement with deadline 2026-08-31. Reporting it as closed would have caused Printec to miss a live XL bid. Status, deadline field and followup corrected.; Ο.Π.Σ.Ε.Α. — Ministry of Citizen Protection integrated security-document issuing system (Hellenic Police ID cards and passports; adjacency: card personalisation and digital identity) — MATERIALLY CONTRADICTED — the most important correction in this set. (1) STATUS: the researcher recorded 'CARRIED-OVER ITEM, STATUS: CLOSED, AWARD PENDING' with an unverified 27/07/2026 deadline. The primary notice exists on TED and I found it by CPV-and-value search: three publications, 348643-2026 (21/05/2026, deadline-receipt-request 25/06/2026), 431349-2026 (24/06/2026, deadline 27/07/2026) and 510892-2026 (23/07/2026, deadline 10/09/2026). The tender is OPEN, not closed, and the correct deadline is 10/09/2026 — a wrong 'closed' status here would have cost a live bid window. (2) NAME: the correct acronym is Ο.Π.Σ.Ε.Α. (Ολοκληρωμένο Πληροφοριακό Σύστημα Εντύπων Ασφαλείας), not 'ΟΨΕΑ'. (3) VALUE: the primary TED figure is EUR 415,648,352 excl. VAT; the press EUR 515.4m is the incl.-VAT gross-up and reconciles exactly at 24% VAT, so both are now quoted with their basis stated. (4) SOURCE TIER upgraded from press to primary, and the 'launched via the Hellenic Corporation of Assets and Participations' attribution dropped — TED records the Ministry of Citizen Protection as contracting authority, full stop. The Toppan incumbency remains press-only and is labelled as such.; Banca Naţională a României (BNR), fiscal code R 361684 — CURRENCY ERROR CORRECTED. Re-fetched live 27/08/2026 via api-pub C_PUBLIC_CANotice/get and GetCANoticeContracts for all three caNoticeIds. The two Wall Street Systems contracts are denominated in EUR, not RON: GetCANoticeContracts returns contractValue 2,471,667 with currency EUR, currencyRate 5.0, defaultCurrencyContractValue 12,358,335 (CAN1168349); and contractValue 2,802,500 with currency EUR, currencyRate 5.1, defaultCurrencyContractValue 14,292,750 (CAN1168935). The notice body confirms it: section2_1_New.totalAcquisitionValue = 2,471,667 with currency localeKey 'EUR'. The researcher therefore made a double error — it read the RON conversion (12,358,335 / 14,292,750) as an 'estimated value' and the EUR contract value as RON, understating each contract 5x. Winner name and VAT SE 556500855301, and contract numbers/dates 186/P/2024 09/12/2024 and 39/P/2026 26/05/2026, are confirmed. CAN1167071 confirmed: MONTRAN SRL RO13018603, RON 477,840 (currency RON, rate 1.0), contract 'CS nr.2/04.05.2026' dated 04/05/2026 — and the object is specifically ReGIS and SaFIR under framework 28/P/2025, sharper than the researcher's 'a BNR payment system'. ADDED: both STP notices use 'negociere fara publicare prealabila', i.e. no competition. opp_size L -> XL because the corrected aggregate is ~EUR 5.4m, but win stays Low: the procedure type makes it closed.; C.N. Poşta Română S.A. (Romanian Post) — CORRECTED on dating and on is_new. Researcher filed this as a 2026 award ('CAN1147177 (11/08/2026) ... estimated RON 27,515,000') with is_new=true. Re-fetched SICAP api-pub C_PUBLIC_CANotice/GetCANoticeContracts for caNoticeId 100647829 (27/08/2026): three contracts, all with contractDate 2025-05-15, winner 'Isitec International' fiscalNumber '442336079' — 140/1079;28 RON 2,665,000, 140/1080;29 RON 12,425,000, 140/1078;27 RON 12,425,000, summing to exactly RON 27,515,000, so the researcher's contract numbers and values are all CORRECT. TED API confirms the underlying award is 320074-2025, published 19/05/2025, buyer C.N. Poşta Română, winner Isitec International, notice total-value RON 27,300,000 (the RON 27,515,000 the researcher called 'estimated' is in fact the sum of the three signed contracts — both figures are now quoted with their sources). The only 2026 events are contract-modification notices (TED notice-type can-modif) on 04/08 and 11/08/2026. Therefore the substance predates the 01/08/2026 baseline by ~15 months: is_new flipped to false and the signal tagged [STANDING — contracts dated 15/05/2025] per rule 7, with source_date kept at 11/08/2026 as the most recent triggering development (the modification notice). Added three further Isitec CNPR awards found on TED this run (402215-2024, 502499-2024, 41463-2026) — rule 3, no winner dropped. Isitec's registration 442336079 confirmed from the SICAP contract record; SICAP does not publish its country of registration, so 'French' is retained from the researcher and is consistent with a 9-digit French SIREN but is not independently confirmed.; Direcţia Generală Regională a Finanţelor Publice Ploieşti (Romanian regional tax directorate) — CEC Bank — Winner CEC Bank SA and the two headline numbers exist, but they were INVERTED: TED records framework-maximum / approximate value RON 4,953,000 and total awarded RON 4,457,700, whereas the signal called RON 4,457,700 the framework maximum. Same error class on 500853-2026: framework maximum is RON 699,672, RON 699,024 is the awarded total. Buyer of 500853/529308 is DGRFP CRAIOVA (id 4416952), not Ploieşti (id RO 2844936). NOT CONFIRMED: the per-treasury mapping 'Mioveni RON 421,560 / Videle RON 551,340 / Lehliu Gară RON 800,640' — those result values appear in the notice but the API returns them unordered against the lot descriptions, so the name-to-value pairing is unverified. Added the 8 lot maxima and the award criteria.; SelfPay (formerly ZebraPay), RO26067497, and Salt Bank, RO10318789 — Values CONFIRMED to the cent, buyer and winner attribution CORRECTED. (a) The researcher put CAN1161203 at 'DGITL Sector 1 Bucharest'. Re-fetched SICAP GetCANoticeList: caNoticeId 100639708, contractingAuthorityNameAndFN '12380248 - DIRECTIA GENERALA DE IMPOZITE SI TAXE LOCALE SECTOR 6'. TED 458394-2026 (03/07/2026) independently names the buyer 'DIRECTIA GENERALA DE IMPOZITE SI TAXE LOCALE SECTOR 6'. It is Sector 6, not Sector 1 — corrected. (b) The researcher attributed these wins to 'SelfPay/Salt Bank' without separating them. GetCANoticeContracts on 100639708 returns 7 contracts summing to exactly RON 6,110,317.82 (matching the researcher), but six are booked to 'SALT BANK' RO10318789 and only call-off 4 to 'SELFPAY' RO26067497 — corrected, and it matters commercially because the contracting party is a bank. (c) SCNA1133771: 2 contracts, RON 1,067,328 to SELFPAY + RON 77,000 to SALT BANK = RON 1,144,328, matching the researcher exactly; buyer confirmed as 'Directia Fiscala a Municipiului Timisoara' (21666630). (d) CAN1166131: 8 contracts totalling RON 1,384,842.50, splitting cleanly into Salt Bank Lot 3 = RON 352,317.50 and Banca Transilvania Lot 2 = RON 1,032,525 — both the researcher's figures (here and in the Banca Transilvania signal) are correct; TED 447350-2026 lists BANCA TRANSILVANIA, SALT BANK and SELFPAY as co-winners. (e) DAN2782089 re-fetched from api-pub DaAwardNoticeCommon/GetDaAwardNoticeList (contractObject='self-service'): 'Inchiriere terminal de tip self-service SelfPay', 17/06/2026, buyer '44246946 DIRECTIA FISCALA LOCALA TARGU MURES', supplier 'RO26067497 SELFPAY', awardedValue 8204.0 — exact match. (f) The researcher's entity label 'Netopia/SelfPay' is NOT supported: an open-web check found no evidence that Netopia owns SelfPay; SelfPay's own site says it was formerly ZebraPay. Entity relabelled. The ~13,000-station figure is a vendor self-claim from selfpay.ro, not a regulator figure, and is flagged as such in the text.; PrivatBank (АТ КБ «ПриватБанк» / JSC CB PrivatBank), EDRPOU 14360570 — Re-pulled every referenced tender from /commercial/ptr/get-ptr.json on 27/08/2026 plus a fresh registry search on query 'POS'. CONFIRMED EXACTLY: 3067542 USD 1,900,000 excl. VAT, bids closed 23/02/2026, award ended 27/04/2026, active.awarded; 3067375 cancelled; 3068908 and 3070386 both unsuccessful; 3071759 UAH 2,010,653 incl. (2) COVERAGE ERROR: the researcher asserted (here and in the CBC 08/2026-15/2026 signal) that a TED buyer-name search on 'Central Bank of Cyprus' returns only coin contracts. It does not. Re-running buyer-name~'CENTRAL BANK OF CYPRUS' on the TED v3 search API on 27/08/2026 returns 17 notices, of which SIX are dated on or after 01/01/2024, and two of those six are the SWIFT-system notices (745708-2024 pin-cfc-standard, 06/12/2024; 667724-2025 can-standard, 10/10/2025). I fetched 667724-2025/xml directly: internal reference 06/2024, CPV 72261000, restricted procedure, lowest price, 1 tender, cbc:TotalAmount currencyID='EUR' 415000, contract IssueDate 2025-09-29, winner ALLIED ENGINEERING GROUP (CYPRUS) LTD. No registration number for the winner is published in the notice. is_new corrected from true to false — both awards are dated 2025, more than 6 months before the run date, with no near-future trigger (playbook rule 7). opp_size changed from L to M to reflect the one Printec-addressable item (EUR 415k SWIFT support), with the EUR 1.16m coin award retained in the text as context only.; Financna uprava RS (FURS - Financial Administration, Ministry of Finance) — The MAIN award is CONFIRMED at primary source (enarocanje obrazecGet?id=975495, re-fetched 27/08/2026): Posta Slovenije reg. 5881447000, EUR 52,245.79 awarded (vrednostSkupna/vrednostIzbrana), EUR 116,101.75 excl. VAT estimate, single bid, contract C1620-26-000004 signed 23/01/2026, notice JN009161/2025-SL2/01 published 27/01/2026 - all exactly as the researcher recorded. TWO CONTRADICTIONS FIXED, both on the device sub-claim: (1) the researcher wrote that FURS 'bought banknote-counting and counterfeit-detection devices TWICE in 2025 (JN 19/2025 and JN 25/2025 BAN)'. It did not. obrazecGet?id=909721 shows JN 19/2025 BAN (JN002110/2025-SL2/01, 07/05/2025) with steviloPonudbe = 0 and idSifOddajaStatus = 1, i.e. ZERO bids and NOT AWARDED; JN 25/2025 BAN was the repeat. Only one purchase happened. (2) PIGO d.o.o. is registered in SKOFLJICA, not Ljubljana (obrazecGet?id=914962). Also added: PIGO's estimate was EUR 7,470 excl. VAT against the EUR 7,560 awarded, the deposit contract's term (01/02/2026-31/01/2029), FURS's full legal name and reg. 2482711000, and the formal reference 430-580/2025 (the researcher quoted the internal label 'JN 40/2025', which is the title, not the reference number). A '%BAN%' sweep of FURS notices confirms no 2026 repeat. is_new CHANGED to false: the trigger is 7 months old with no near-future deadline, so per the recency rule this is CONTEXT and now carries [STANDING - dated 2026]. keep = true - the item is real and correctly sourced, only the device narrative was wrong.; Banka e Shqipërisë (Bank of Albania) — CONTRADICTED on its central factual claim, and corrected. The researcher wrote that this tender 'has DROPPED OFF the Bank of Albania live tender list (13 notices, none of them this one)'. That is FALSE. On 27/08/2026 I paginated the list (page 1 = 10 notices at ?rpp=1, page 2 = 3 notices at ?rpp=2; the page itself prints '1 deri 10 nga 13') and the banknote plastic-packaging tender IS one of the 13, sitting on page 2. The researcher appears to have read only page 1 and inferred absence. The underlying inference (tender closed, no winner published) still holds, but it holds because the DEADLINE PASSED on 18/08/2026, not because the notice was removed — Bank of Albania leaves expired notices published (7 of the 13 are already expired). CORRECTED AND ADDED from the primary notice page re-fetched this run: fond limit ALL 17,511,600, which the researcher never captured, and the procedure type 'national open tender accompanied by direct invitations to foreign candidates', which is the commercially important fact because it means BoA proactively invites foreign vendors for cash-centre equipment. source_date moved from 27/08/2026 (mere observation date) to 18/08/2026 (the triggering event, the deadline passing). is_new set to FALSE — the signal itself is flagged 'STATUS of baseline item', so it was known at the 01/08/2026 baseline. Also confirmed the researcher's claim that BoA publishes no award notices: the only outcome-type link on the tender section is 'Shoqëritë e përjashtuara nga procedurat e prokurimit' (companies excluded from procurement).; Oesterreichische Nationalbank (OeNB) / Brink's Global Services Deutschland GmbH; Colt Technology Services GmbH; AT&T Global Network Services Belgium Luxembourg SPRL — CONTRADICTED on the researcher's claim that the values were 'not published ... stated as a gap'. The values ARE published in both TED notices and were retrieved on re-check: Brink's EUR 433,235 (TED 785575-2025 XML, PayableAmount, contract date 2025-11-25, framework call-off type fa-wo-rc); Colt EUR 112,800 and AT&T EUR 112,887.60 as two separate lots (TED 869270-2025 XML, contract date 2025-12-11). Also corrected the AT&T legal entity: the notice names 'AT&T Global Network Services Belgium Luxembourg SPRL', not 'AT&T Global Network Services'. This was a research gap, not a fabrication, so the row is kept with the figures supplied. opp_size moved Unscoped -> S now that the values are known; is_new set false (both are 2025 items with no 2026 trigger).; Posta Slovenije d.o.o. (Post of Slovenia) — Re-fetched JN006554/2026-EUe17/01 (idObrazec 1148141), JN005236/2026-EUe17/01 (idObrazec 1126055), JN006156/2025-EUe30/01 (idObrazec 1127590) and both dossiers (idDosje 646467 and 634939) on 27/08/2026. CONFIRMED: the user-equipment notice, TED 569790-2026, ref JN-78132, published 18/08/2026, requests to participate 17/09/2026 12:00, questions 04/09/2026, four lots, no published estimate, no corrigendum. CONTRADICTED (1): the researcher listed 'Komunikacijska oprema 2026' as 'also open' - it is not; its request-to-participate deadline was 31/07/2026 12:00 and expired 27 days before the run date, and the dossier contains only the original notice. CONTRADICTED (2): the researcher quoted the EAE Solutions parcel-sorter contract at 'EUR 8,262,492'; the primary award notice records EUR 6,772,535.00 excl. VAT (the EUR 8,262,492.70 register figure is the same sum incl. 22% VAT) against a EUR 6,825,000 estimate, 2 bids / 1 admissible, framework 6600002809/JNB of 11/06/2026, TED 467778-2026 published 07/07/2026. ADDED: the lot names, the two-stage nature of both utilities procedures, the TED id for the sorter, and the fact that the winner has no Slovenian registration number published (foreign entity). ACCESS: the human-facing canonical link https://www.enarocanje.si/pregled-objav/1148141 is a Vue SPA route that 404s to non-browser clients even with a desktop Chrome User-Agent; it was re-fetched this run via the site's own public JSON API at https://www.enarocanje.si/api/obrazec/objava/obrazecGet?id=1148141 (no authentication, desktop Chrome UA), which is the same record.; Central Bank of Cyprus — Tenders 08/2026 (AML on-site inspection support) and 15/2026 (HR consultancy) — The signal's PRIMARY claim is confirmed: I re-fetched https://www.centralbank.cy/en/the-bank/tenders on 27/08/2026 with a desktop Chrome User-Agent (HTTP 200) and Tender 12/2026 is the only tender listed — no 08/2026, no 15/2026, no archive. But a SUPPORTING claim is factually wrong and is corrected here: the researcher stated that a TED buyer-name search on 'Central Bank of Cyprus' from 01/01/2024 'returns only FOUR notices, all coin contracts (150921-2025, 678104-2025, 477731-2026, 557762-2026)'. Re-running buyer-name~'CENTRAL BANK OF CYPRUS' against the TED v3 search API today returns 17 notices, six of them dated 01/01/2024 or later: 745708-2024 (06/12/2024, pin-cfc-standard, SWIFT), 150921-2025, 667724-2025 (10/10/2025, award, SWIFT, winner ALLIED ENGINEERING GROUP (CYPRUS) LTD, EUR 415,000), 678104-2025, 477731-2026, 557762-2026. The corrected count does not change the conclusion for 08/2026 and 15/2026 — neither appears on TED under any notice type — so keep=true and the item stands as an unresolved disclosure gap. confidence stays Low: the outcomes of both tenders remain unconfirmed by any source. No winner, value or deadline has been invented. Route that failed: web.archive.org is blocked to this tool, so the deleted CBC notice pages could not be recovered from a snapshot.; jnportal.ujn.gov.rs (Serbian Public Procurement Portal) — Re-ran the sweep myself today against the same two grids (TenderNotices and VContractRegisterPublic) via the portal API. CONFIRMED: notices total 761,626 — exactly the researcher's figure; 'банкомат' and 'bankomat' both return ZERO notices; CPV 32442000 returns exactly ONE 2026 contract, the NBS/Payten POS terminals at RSD 150,000 (PIB 110428879 correct, contract Г.бр. 3385); and the posted.co.rs non-ZJN status underpinning the structural argument. TWO CORRECTIONS, hence 'contradicted' rather than 'confirmed': (1) the claim of 'ZERO ATM procurement of any kind' in the contract register is not literally true — there is one, 'Услуге померања банкомата' (ATM relocation), 13/06/2024, RSD 11,707.69, winner Miodrag Brkić PR / M.B. Tim Beograd PIB 103692783. It is a removals job and does not disturb the structural conclusion, but the absolute claim needed fixing and playbook rule 3 requires the winner be recorded. (2) The researcher said CPV 32442000 'returns exactly ONE contract in 2026' — true — but presented it as if it were the whole NBS terminal picture. The full CPV 32442000 set is 6 contracts and includes NBS 'Мобилни терминали', 25/11/2025, RSD 4,080,000 to Info-Kod Rešenja d.o.o. PIB 106780962 — a materially larger NBS terminal supplier that was missed and that contradicts the 'Payten is inside NBS' framing being the whole story. Also note the contract-register total is now 1,674,409, not the researcher's 1,674,170 — the register is written continuously, so that delta is drift within the same day, not an error. The core structural finding STANDS and is the most useful item in this batch.; Oesterreichische Banknoten- und Sicherheitsdruck GmbH (OeBS) — CONTRADICTED on 'No estimated value is published for any category'. All three TED XMLs carry <cbc:EstimatedOverallContractAmount currencyID="EUR">10000000</cbc:EstimatedOverallContractAmount> inside RequestedTenderTotal — verified by printing the surrounding XML for 582804-2026 (project ref Akt.Nr. G15146069-2). Deadline 05/10/2026 and dps-list contracting-system type confirmed; dispatch date is 21/08/2026 (researcher said USP 23/08/2026). The OeBS award list was independently re-verified via a TED buyer sweep (67 notices) and every cited notice id and date matched; the winners' values, which the researcher omitted, were retrieved and added — notably PARVIS at EUR 1,945,430, an L-band award. opp_size deliberately left Unscoped despite the EUR 30m DPS because none of it is Printec-addressable; raising the band would corrupt ranking.; Cyprus commercial banks and eprocurement.gov.cy coverage (structural) — The structural core is CONFIRMED: the CBC tenders page re-fetched 27/08/2026 states verbatim that the CBC contracts under its own Directives (harmonised with 2014/24/EU), publishes in the Official Gazette and on its website, and reaches the EU Official Journal only above threshold. The eprocurement.gov.cy register and detail pages were re-fetched today and do render bidder names and prices without a login (session cookie only), so claim (b) about competitor intelligence is confirmed by direct use. CONTRADICTED on one product category: the researcher listed 'payments modernization (instant payments, ISO 20022, RTGS, digital euro) — NOTHING'. A TED buyer-name search on 'CENTRAL BANK OF CYPRUS' run today surfaces the CBC SWIFT-system administration/maintenance/support contract — call for competition 745708-2024 (06/12/2024) and award 667724-2025 (10/10/2025), winner ALLIED ENGINEERING GROUP (CYPRUS) LTD, EUR 415,000, CPV 72261000. SWIFT is payment-messaging infrastructure, so the payments category is not empty and the signal text is corrected. NOT INDEPENDENTLY RE-VERIFIED: the '18,330 records searched by title and buyer' figure and the exhaustiveness of the Greek/English keyword sweep — I re-ran only targeted buyer-name and full-text queries, so the ZERO for ATM/POS/kiosk/HSM/fraud rests on the previous run's sweep, not on mine. Also surfaced while verifying and NOT part of this batch: Treasury of the Republic of Cyprus tender 'MODERNIZATION OF THE NATIONAL LOTTERY OF CYPRUS', TED 508629-2026, published 23/07/2026, deadline 18/09/2026 — flagged in followup as an unassessed lead.
- **Unconfirmed (single/weak source, kept at Low) (3):** Slovak merchants / acquirers (Act 384/2025 Z. z. cashless-acceptance mandate, as postponed by Act 30/2026 Z. z.), Centralna banka Crne Gore (CBCG, Central Bank of Montenegro), JCC Payment Systems Ltd (Cyprus bank-owned card processor) / Υφυπουργείο Έρευνας, Καινοτομίας και Ψηφιακής Πολιτικής (Deputy Ministry of Research, Innovation and Digital Policy)


## Access issues & operator requests (per procurement portal)

| procurement portal | Access issue / what failed & the fallback used |
|---|---|
| TED (EU-wide) | No blocks encountered. TED Search API v3 (POST https://api.ted.europa.eu/v3/notices/search) answered every query without authentication. All notice XMLs were fetched from https://ted.europa.eu/en/notice/<id>/xml with a desktop Chrome User-Agent header and returned full eForms UBL. ONE partial failure and its fallback, worth recording as a general technique: https://ted.europa.eu/en/notice/511411-2026/xml (Magyar Nemzeti Bank HSM tender) returned an empty/unparseable body on the EN route. I retried the SAME notice on the Hungarian language path https://ted.europa.eu/hu/notice/511411-2026/xml with the same desktop User-Agent and got the full 37.5 KB XML — which is the source of the Entrust nShield 5c mid specification, the 36-month manufacturer-support term and the 24/08/2026 deadline. When a TED notice XML comes back empty on /en/, retry on the notice's filing-language path. The TED API's notice-title~ operator performs fuzzy multilingual expansion and returned large volumes of irrelevant historic notices (a 'bankovky' query surfaced 2016-2017 Belgian and French banknote-printing notices). I therefore treated title queries as a discovery aid only and confirmed every kept result against its own XML. Buyer-name and CPV queries were reliable. No CAPTCHA was encountered and none was attempted; no login wall was worked around; no PDF extraction fallback was needed because the eForms XML route worked throughout. |
| Greece — Diavgeia / KIMDIS / ESIDIS | KIMDIS/ESIDIS DOWN: https://cerpp.eprocurement.gov.gr/kimds2/ and .../unprotected/searchNotice.htm both returned HTTP 503 with a WebLogic bridge error ("No backend server available for connection") on repeated attempts with a desktop Chrome User-Agent — a server-side outage, not a block. Fallback used: the Diavgeia openData API, which is adequate because every KIMDIS-registered Greek notice also carries a Diavgeia ADA. promitheus.gov.gr 302-redirects to portal.eprocurement.gov.gr as expected; the deep JSPX search page returned 404, so it was not usable this run. contracts.gr: the per-CPV and per-organisation public pages relied on last run are GONE — the site is now a login-gated SaaS. https://www.contracts.gr/ loads (HTTP 200) but /search, /cpv/{code} and all query variants return 404, and the only links on the landing page are marketing / pricing / free-trial routes. Substituted with the TED search API plus Diavgeia openData. TED search API: field names are restrictive — queries using classification-cpv, cpv=, FT~ or notice-title~ all return HTTP 400, and the field "deadline-receipt-tender" is invalid; only "deadline-receipt-request" works. Workaround used: fetch all Greek notices for the window and filter CPVs and keywords locally. Also hit HTTP 429 at page 13 of the June sweep; a re-run with backoff and a narrowed date range completed 10/07–27/08 in full (1,344 of 1,344). bankofgreece.gr/enimerosi/diagonismoi and /kyria-themata/diagwnismoi both return 404; the working route is the announcements search filtered to the tender category, which was fetched successfully. Systemic-bank supplier pages: nbg.gr/en/group/procurement 404, eurobank.gr/en/group/suppliers 404, alpha.gr/en/omilos/promitheies HTTP 500 — these are absences of published procurement, not access blocks. pdftotext is not installed on this machine; PDF text was extracted with pdfminer.six instead. No CAPTCHA was encountered and no login wall was worked around. |
| Romania — SEAP/SICAP (e-licitatie) | 1) https://www.e-licitatie.ro/api-pub/... — GET returns {"message":"The requested resource does not support http method 'GET'"}; all list endpoints are POST. With Referer + Origin + desktop Chrome UA there was NO 403 at any point this run (the documented 'Referrer cannot be null' guard was satisfied). 2) Unfiltered GetCNoticeList/GetCANoticeList silently cap at total=3000 sorted ASCENDING from 2013, i.e. an unfiltered pull returns only 2019-2020 rows and would have produced a false 'nothing found'. Worked around with weekly startPublicationDate/endPublicationDate windows (17 windows per endpoint). 3) Sorting parameters (sortColumn, sortDirection, sortColumnsList) are accepted and IGNORED; publicationDateStart/publicationDateFrom/noticeStateDateStart/pubDateStart are accepted and IGNORED on the two notice-list endpoints — only startPublicationDate/endPublicationDate work there, while the direct-acquisition endpoint uses publicationDateStart instead. Documented so the next run does not re-derive it. 4) The notice DETAIL endpoints are not discoverable from the main bundles: /api-pub/{PublicNotice,NoticeCommon,CANotice,PublicCANotice,CANoticeCommon,ENotice}/GetCANotice all return 'No HTTP resource was found'. Resolved by fetching the lazily-loaded Angular module at https://www.e-licitatie.ro/js/pub/notices/ca-notice/service (note the /js prefix and NO .js suffix — /pub/... returns the SPA shell and /pub/....js returns a 500 redirect to /assets/errors/internal-error.htm), which named the four *_PUBLIC_CANotice controllers. 5) GetCANoticeContracts returns ["Eroare de sistem ...","Value cannot be null. Parameter name: collection"] unless the FULL filter object is sent including sortOrder:[] and the four from/to range objects. 6) https://www.cec.ro/achizitii — as documented, needs a desktop Chrome User-Agent; retrieved successfully, content is Drupal-rendered and required local HTML-to-text extraction. Content is genuinely a single stale item, not a fetch failure. 7) No CAPTCHA was encountered and no login wall was bypassed. No source was dropped silently. |
| Bulgaria — CAIS EOP (app.eop.bg) | app.eop.bg — /buyer/11700, /buyer/14878, /today and /api/v1/public/buyer/11700 tried with a desktop Chrome User-Agent: all HTTP 200 but all return the SAME 7,500-byte Angular JS shell (F5/BIG-IP WAF plus oidc-client auth). No backend JSON found. FALLBACK USED: TED Search API v3 plus ted.europa.eu/en/notice/<id>/xml, which carries the CAIS EOP/AOP internal procedure ids (BNB 578429, 588322, 572382, 255873; Варна 580142) so notices can still be matched back to EOP. www.bnb.bg/AboutUs/AUPublicProcurements/AUPPList/index.htm — reachable with a desktop UA and read successfully, but it only lists BNB procedures up to 2020; the page itself carries a notice that procurement after 15/06/2020 is conducted through CAIS EOP. BNB below-threshold lots after 2020 are therefore NOT retrievable from bnb.bg. www.bnb.bg/AboutUs/AUPublicProcurement/index.htm — BNB system error page ("Възникна системна грешка! Несъществуващ адрес!"); the correct path was recovered from a homepage link and used instead. data.egov.bg organisation profile — HTTP 403; the АОП/ROP open-data REST API requires a registered api_key we do not hold, so below-TED-threshold Bulgarian lots were NOT swept this run. banker.bg (Banking Today 2026 article, 27/03/2026) — Cloudflare JS interstitial on both WebFetch (HTTP 403) and curl with a desktop UA ("Just a moment... Enable JavaScript and cookies to continue"); not used, and its reported EUR 200m euro-transition investment figure is therefore not stated as fact anywhere above. TED Search API v3 — an initial call failed with HTTP 400 on unsupported `fields` values; resolved by enumerating the API's own supported-field list from the error body and re-querying with valid names. No CAPTCHA was attempted and no login wall was worked around. |
| Serbia — jnportal.ujn.gov.rs | jnportal.ujn.gov.rs/api/* behaved exactly as the brief warned: 401 with no session cookie, 403 with a cookie but no UserToken header, and — a further trap — 400 Bad Request on plausible-but-wrong route names (api/PublicNotices) and 405 on POST, which look like dead ends but are not. Route discovery required the Browser pane network log; guessing endpoint names does not work. Claude-in-Chrome was NOT connected (list_connected_browsers returned empty), so the built-in Browser pane (mcp__Claude_Browser__preview_start / read_network_requests / javascript_tool) was used instead. The /oglasi-svi page also ignores the ?initFilter= URL parameter when loaded headlessly, so all filtering was done directly against the API. www.posted.co.rs/javne-nabavke/ and /o-nama/javne-nabavke/ both return 404; the live path is https://www.posted.co.rs/o-nama/nabavke.html (found by parsing the homepage) and it served fine to a desktop Chrome User-Agent. pdftotext is not installed on this machine; all PDF text was extracted with pdfminer.six. Notice PDFs are Cyrillic and the portal transliterates Latin brand names into Cyrillic in some fields ('Глорy УW-Ф4' = Glory UW-F4, 'Паyтен' = Payten, 'еxцеет Цард Сцхwеиз АГ' = exceet Card Schweiz AG, 'Вивеx Гроуп' = Vivex Group, 'Кессинг' = Keesing) — brand names have been de-transliterated in the signals and the original Cyrillic kept where it aids re-finding. No CAPTCHA was encountered and no login wall was bypassed. |
| Ukraine — Prozorro (prozorro.gov.ua + tender.privatbank.ua) | 1) prozorro.gov.ua/tender/<friendlyID> is a Vue SPA (842-byte shell) — confirmed. https://public-api.prozorro.gov.ua/api/2.5/tenders/UA-2026-08-26-012958-a returns {"status":"error","name":"tender_id","description":"Not Found"} because the CDB only accepts the 32-hex UUID — confirmed. WORKAROUND USED: https://clarity-project.info/tender/<friendlyID> with a desktop Chrome User-Agent returns full server-rendered HTML (HTTP 200, ~35KB) including the UUID and the winner; used for every award reported. Dates are in <span class="date" data-timestamp="..."> attributes and must be converted from unix time. 2) prozorro.gov.ua/api/search/tenders POST rejects {"query":...} and {"tenderID":...} (silently returns an unfiltered 10,000-row feed rather than an error) and rejects page=0 with "The page must be at least 1". Only {"text":...,"page":>=1} filters correctly, and multi-word text is ANDed so long phrases legitimately return 0 — use 1-2 word terms. 3) BLOCKED — tender.privatbank.ua/commercial/tender/PB-2026-03-17-3068329 returns a 151KB AngularJS 1.6 shell with no data (HTTP 200). Reverse-engineered the data route from /commercial/js/pb/tender-info/tic-info-root-pb.js to GET /commercial/ptr/get-ptr.json?prozorro=&own-app=&hash=<PB-id>; it requires a JSESSIONID cookie plus the X-CSRF-TOKEN embedded in the page HTML, and even with both supplied it returns {"status":-1,"errMsg":"Помилка обробки запиту"}. /commercial/spring/ad/getAdInfo.json is 404; POST to get-ptr.json returns "Request method 'POST' not supported". Escalated to Claude-in-Chrome per the playbook: mcp list_connected_browsers returned [] — NO browser is connected to this session, so the escalation path was unavailable. No CAPTCHA was attempted and no login wall was worked around. Logged in operator_requests. 4) dozorro.org, api.dozorro.org and bi.prozorro.gov.ua are behind Cloudflare interstitials ("Just a moment...", HTTP 403/000) with a desktop UA and were not usable; not needed once clarity-project.info worked. 5) No source was dropped silently; every URL tried and every fallback used is named above. |
| Croatia — EOJN (eojn.hr) | eojn.hr — homepage and /procurements-public return HTTP 200 with a desktop Chrome User-Agent (better than the source catalogue's recorded HTTP 500 for the deep link) but serve a pure SPA shell with no server-rendered rows (zero <tr>); the backing endpoint https://eojn.hr/api/postupci returns HTTP 401. No login was attempted and no bot protection or CAPTCHA was bypassed. Fallback used: TED Search API v3 plus locally extracted buyer procurement-plan PDFs. https://www.hnb.hr/documents/20182/121306/h-plan-nabave-hnb-2026.pdf — fetched successfully (HTTP 200, 842,185 bytes, 17 pages) with a desktop Chrome UA; pdftotext is not installed on this machine, so text was extracted with pdfminer.six and re-assembled into table rows by x/y coordinates before mapping ref codes to subjects. https://www.dkom.hr/javna-objava-odluka/10 — HTTP 200 with a desktop UA, but the page returned is a Google Custom Search wrapper rather than a server-rendered decisions table; the appeal register at /upisnik/9 was NOT mined this run and no DKOM decision is cited anywhere in these findings. https://eojn.nn.hr/SPIN/application/ipn/Oglasnik/PreuzimanjeUgovoraOCD.aspx — HTTP 200, confirmed reachable, but the ASP.NET __VIEWSTATE postback download was not executed this run for time reasons. TED Search API v3 (https://api.ted.europa.eu/v3/notices/search) worked unauthenticated throughout. Nothing was silently dropped. |
| Slovenia — e-narocanje | enarocanje.si /pregled-objav/<id> returns HTTP 404 to non-browser clients (Vue SPA with client-side routing) — confirmed still 404 with a desktop Chrome User-Agent, so the UA retry alone does NOT defeat it. Resolved by reading the SPA bundle https://www.enarocanje.si/assets/index-eeafb3e1.js and its lazy chunks (Objava-59e3c1a8.js, Pogodba-c7d2003d.js, Evidencna-80cdeab5.js, EvidencnoNarocilo-*.js) to recover the API contract, then calling https://www.enarocanje.si/api/ directly with a desktop Chrome UA — every endpoint returned 200 without authentication. Bare /api/ and guessed paths (/api/pregledobjav) return 404; only exact endpoint names work. The `naslov` filter is a prefix match unless the term is wrapped in % wildcards — an early sweep without wildcards produced false negatives (e.g. 'sortirn' returned nothing) and was redone with wildcards. Decision PDFs at /api/datoteka/get?id=<base64> downloaded fine and were text-extracted locally with pdfminer.six. The evidencna-narocila detail endpoints (evidencnaNarocila/vnos/*) sit behind a login and were NOT used or worked around; the public evidencna data is only an annual aggregate report (2025 reporting period closed 07/03/2026), so below-threshold award detail was taken from the public contracts-register CSV instead. No CAPTCHA was encountered or attempted. No source was dropped. |
| Slovakia — UVO (uvo.gov.sk) | 1) https://eforms.uvo.gov.sk/ (and /api/notices, /notices, /vestnik) all 302 to a Keycloak login at iam.uvo.gov.sk — LOGIN WALL; not worked around, logged here as instructed. 2) https://opendata.uvo.gov.sk/ failed to resolve (curl HTTP 000). 3) /vyhladavanie/vyhladavanie-zakaziek/detail and several guessed search paths (vyhladavanie-oznameni, vyhladavanie-vestnik, vestnik, vyhladavanie-zmluv, vyhladavanie-referencii) 302 to /errors/error404.html, confirming the baseline note that /openapi/ and /OpenData/ are dead. 4) https://josephine.proebiz.com/sk/tender/search returns 404 — the JOSEPHINE e-procurement platform used by NBS and Slovenská pošta has no public search route at that path; bid documents there require registration. 5) All working fetches used a desktop Chrome User-Agent; the ÚVO Vestník search and notice-detail pages returned 200 with full server-rendered HTML under that UA, so no fallback to cached snippets or press was needed for any ÚVO row — the only press-tier source in this file is the Act 384/2025 explainer. 6) The result-row pageNo parameter did not paginate on GET (requesting page 2 returned page 1), so listings over 20 rows were split by narrowing dzOd/dzDo instead — recorded so a future run does not mistake this for missing data. |
| Czechia — NEN (nen.nipez.cz) + ČNB E-ZAK (ezak.cnb.cz) | ezak.cnb.cz: /verejne-zakazky and /zakazky return 404/301; the working GET route is https://ezak.cnb.cz/contract_index.html with ?page=N pagination, confirmed with a desktop Chrome User-Agent. E-ZAK contract detail pages do NOT publish the winning supplier or the contract value for ANY phase, including 'Zadáno' (awarded) — values had to be recovered from the parallel TED notice, which is how the ČNB manipulační-technika per-lot figures were obtained, and the ČNB HSM winner could not be recovered at all. nen.nipez.cz: the p:vz: filter path works for GET keyword search (e.g. /verejne-zakazky/p:vz:nazev=HSM); a plain ?nazev= query string is silently IGNORED and returns the unfiltered default list of 50 rows — a trap that would produce false negatives for anyone scripting it. Result sets are capped at 50 rows per query with no visible pagination control, so high-frequency terms (pokladn, trezor, platebn, terminál) were truncated; narrower terms were used to compensate. smlouvy.gov.cz (Czech contracts register, used to try to recover the ČNB HSM winner E-ZAK withholds): the quick search ignores subject_idnum and returns the unfiltered 4,762-row list; the advanced search returns HTTP 500 on a GET with the documented parameter names (subject_idnum, contract_descr, search_type) and appears to require a POST with a session token. Not solved this run — logged rather than dropped, and escalated in operator_requests. TED API v3: several field names (winner-decision-date, description-lot, estimated-value-lot) are rejected with HTTP 400; the reliable route is a minimal field list for discovery, then fetching https://ted.europa.eu/en/notice/{id}/xml and parsing UBL locally. The WebFetch tool on TED /pdf routes returned unusable or fabricated content for two notices — for 504116-2026 it described a vehicle purchase that is not in the notice — so all figures in this report come from the /xml route, which is authoritative. One /xml fetch (69049-2026) returned HTTP 429 rate-limit; its values were taken from the API search result instead. pdftotext is not installed on this machine (pdfminer.six and pypdf are). tenderarena.cz was deliberately NOT retried — the brief records it as returning only a 1.5 KB JSF shell; the proven TED buyer-scoped query on 'Česká pošta' was used instead and yielded 412 notices. No paywalls, no login walls and no CAPTCHAs were encountered on any Czech portal this run. |
| Hungary — EKR (ekr.gov.hu / kozbeszerzes.hu) | ekr.gov.hu/portal/* returns the Angular index.html for EVERY path including the JS bundles (SPA catch-all); the fix was to fetch assets and API from the ROOT host (https://ekr.gov.hu/main.<hash>.bundle.js, https://ekr.gov.hu/api/...). https://ekr.gov.hu/ekr-em/... 302-redirects back to /portal. All requests were sent with a desktop Chrome User-Agent; no 403s, no CAPTCHAs and no login wall were encountered — the entire notice corpus and every notice PDF are public. The EKR opendata result-report URL (/api/publikus/eredmeny-tajekoztato-hirdetmenyek-url/hu?id=...) resolves to a 7 MB SeekTable JavaScript report yielding no extractable text — the notice PDF route was used instead for every award. dontobizottsag.kozbeszerzes.hu (procurement review board) was searched via the open web for any 2026 challenge to the SimplePay/BKK/MÁK awards; nothing found, so no review-board signal is claimed. |

EU Funding & Tenders Portal: the tender-details SPA route for EC-ENEST/TGD/2026/EA-LOP/0101 would not render in the browser pane and the SEDIA search API returned HTTP 500. The CBCG cash-equipment tender is therefore cited to press and kept at Low confidence pending the primary notice. | Bank-owned procurement pages | MAJOR UNLOCK, reusable: tender.privatbank.ua is an AngularJS SPA whose search API previously rejected headless calls. Route that works with plain curl and a desktop Chrome User-Agent: (1) GET https://tender.privatbank.ua/commercial/ to obtain a JSESSIONID cookie scoped to /commercial; (2) GET https://tender.privatbank.ua/commercial/tender and read the Spring CSRF token out of the inline script line `var csrf = {name: "_csrf", headerName: "X-CSRF-TOKEN", value: "<uuid>"};`; (3) POST that cookie plus header X-CSRF-TOKEN to https://tender.privatbank.ua/commercial/v2/registry/main/search with body {"query":"<term>","rows":50,"start":0} — the parameter is `query`, NOT `keyword`/`text`/`search`/`searchText` (those are ignored and silently return the whole 4,124-record corpus). Response fields include prozorroId, tenderName, trStatus, ptrAmount/ptrCurrency/ptrTax, publishDate, enquiryEnd, tradeBeg, awardEnd, cpvList, itemNameList, lotNameList, procurementMethodType and okpo. (4) Per-tender detail: GET https://tender.privatbank.ua/commercial/ptr/get-ptr.json?ad-id=<numeric adId> — the parameter is `ad-id` WITH A HYPHEN; `adId`, `id` and `ptr-hash` all fail. It returns lots, items with full technical-specification text, CPV, document titles and all periods. HARD LIMIT: the winner is NOT in any public PrivatBank endpoint. /commercial/ptr/ad-bids.json returns {"message":"Вам необхідно авторизуватися"} (login required) and get-ptr.json has no awards or bids node — supplier names require a supplier-cabinet login, which was NOT used (login wall, not worked around). Other blocks and fallbacks: prozorro.gov.ua/api/search/tenders needs POST (GET returns 405) and returns no tender UUID, so friendly-ID→UUID resolution for the openprocurement CDB remains unavailable; zakupki.prom.ua is behind Cloudflare (403); smarttender.biz search 404. www.cec.ro/achizitii and www.posted.co.rs both returned 200 directly with the desktop Chrome UA (no 403 this run, unlike the previous run). www.posted.co.rs/javne-nabavke is a 404 — the working path is /o-nama/nabavke.html. www.posta.hr/nabava redirects to the homepage — the working paths are hrvatska.posta.hr/hr/javna-nabava and .../nabava-na-koju-se-ne-primjenjuje-zakon-o-javnoj-nabavi-296/296. www.elta.gr/diagonismoi and /el/diagonismoi are 404s; the working paths are /declarations/promitheia-ylikon and /declarations/parochi-ypiresion, and the document hrefs in that HTML contain escaped backslashes that must be stripped before fetching from media.elta.gr. www.posta.ba/tenderi is a 404; the working path is /javne-nabavke/. Kosovo/Albania commercial-bank tender pages probed and NOT found (404/500): bkt-ks.com/en/tenders, bkt-ks.com/tenderet, procreditbank-kos.com/en/tenders, nlb-kos.com/tenderet, raiffeisen-kosovo.com/en/tenders; bpb.ba/tenderi did not resolve; posta.si/javna-narocila, posta.sk/informacie/verejne-obstaravanie and bgpost.bg/bg/1046 are 404s; posta.hu/kozerdeku_adatok/beszerzesek loads but exposes only navigation (Magyar Posta publishes via EKR). PDFs were extracted locally with pdftotext/pdfminer, not via the fetch tool. Claude-in-Chrome was checked (list_connected_browsers returned an empty list) so no browser escalation was possible. No CAPTCHA was encountered and no login wall was bypassed. |
| Austria — ausschreibungen.usp.gv.at (OeNB & public buyers) | gv.vergabeportal.at (ANKÖ) — the e-submission platform behind both the GSA recycler framework (Detail/244277) and the Münze weighing line (Detail/252147) — is an AngularJS single-page app that returns only a 3,966-byte shell to curl even with a desktop Chrome User-Agent, and it fronts its content with hCaptcha (js.hcaptcha.com is loaded on every page). Per the playbook I did NOT attempt to solve or bypass the CAPTCHA, and I did not probe for a way around it. Fallback route used and successful: the same procedures are published in full on TED (the primary EU notice), so I read the German TED HTML at https://ted.europa.eu/de/notice/<id>/html for 305531-2026, 588745-2026, 18441-2026 and 157688-2026, and used the TED Search API v3 (POST https://api.ted.europa.eu/v3/notices/search) for the buyer-name sweeps. Nothing was dropped as a result. Endpoints probed and rejected before finding the right one, logged for the next run: /public/api/tender/<id>, /public/tender/<id>, /public/api/tenderdetail?id=, /public/api/tender?id= all return 404 on USP; on ANKÖ, /api/Detail/<id> returns 404 and /Detail/GetDetail?id= and /Ausschreibungen/Detail/<id> both return the same Angular shell. USP itself served everything over plain curl with a desktop Chrome User-Agent — no 403s, no rate limiting, no JS wall on the JSON API or the detail pages. One self-inflicted issue worth recording: my first HTML-to-text filter stripped page content along with the <script> blocks and silently produced empty output for five detail pages, which briefly looked like server throttling. It was not. Corrected with a sed-based extractor bounded by id="content" and id="footer", and all five pages were re-fetched successfully. Data-quality caveat logged rather than smoothed over: the two Österreichische Post notices carry titles ("Überholung kleiner 5 Mio. Banknoten", "AKT AST-7016 Fullservice 2026") that do not match their own body description ("Abruf aus RV: Automatische Kassentresore klein und groß", framework dated 04/08/2015). Both fields are reported verbatim in the signal; the 04/08/2015 date is the parent framework's conclusion date exactly as published, not the date of the call-off. |

NOT attempted, per the no-CAPTCHA rule. Fallback used: the ungated POST search on /epps/common/viewOpenedTenders.do. Consequence: I can see every tender whose deadline has PASSED (with all bidders and prices) but NOT the currently-open pipeline from the portal itself — open items were recovered from TED instead. 2) https://www.eprocurement.gov.cy/epps/cft/prepareViewCfTWS.do?resourceId=N returned HTTP 200 with an EMPTY body on a plain fetch (htmx-driven page); fixed by seeding a JSESSIONID from the register page with curl -c/-b plus a desktop Chrome User-Agent and a Referer header. 3) https://www.centralbank.cy/en/tenders and https://www.centralbank.cy/el/prosforodotiseis both redirect to easyconsole.cfm?id=46, a 404 page; the working path is https://www.centralbank.cy/en/the-bank/tenders and it required the desktop Chrome User-Agent (as the brief predicted). It also rejects some CPV codes in expert search (e.g. 51219000) with QUERY_UNSUPPORTED_FIELD_VALUE, so full-text (FT ~) and buyer-name queries were used instead. 6) TED PDF renderings of award notices (e.g. https://ted.europa.eu/en/notice/525822-2026/pdf) returned almost no substantive fields through the fetch tool; the /xml route downloaded with a Chrome User-Agent and parsed locally gave the full data (winner, payable amount, tender count, national reference, procedure type) and is the route to use for every TED notice. No source was silently dropped. |

**Operator requests — gated / paywalled / blocked sources to fetch or verify manually:**
- **TED (EU-wide):** none — every source needed this run was reachable without gating, paywall or login. All figures come from openly published eForms XML on ted.europa.eu.
- **Greece — Diavgeia / KIMDIS / ESIDIS:** 1) ΟΔΑΠ tender "Β' Φάση εφαρμογής ψηφιακών υπηρεσιών εξυπηρέτησης επισκεπτών σε Αρχαιολογικούς Χώρους και Μουσεία", TED 408136-2026, EUR 30,013,782.92, closed 23/07/2026: the full διακήρυξη with its technical annexes sits behind the ESIDIS / promitheus.gov.gr electronic-tendering portal, which requires an account. An operator with an ESIDIS login should download the διακήρυξη and the Παράρτημα technical specifications to establish how many self-service ticketing kiosks and payment terminals are in scope, and which are Phase A carry-over versus the 17 new sites. 2) KIMDIS (cerpp.eprocurement.gov.gr/kimds2) was returning HTTP 503 throughout this run. If it is back up, an operator should re-run a below-threshold search for Greece on CPV 30132200, 30123600, 30144200 and 48810000 to confirm nothing was missed behind the outage. 3) The OPSEA / Ministry of Citizen Protection EUR 515.4m ID-and-passport tender: the primary notice and any extension decision could not be opened, and the 27/07/2026 deadline is press-sourced only. An operator should retrieve the primary ESIDIS/TED notice to confirm the deadline and the list of qualified consortia. 4) No paywalled PDF blocked this run beyond the above — but contracts.gr is now subscription-only. If Printec holds or buys a contracts.gr subscription it would restore the per-CPV Greek below-threshold monitoring that this beat lost this cycle.
- **Romania — SEAP/SICAP (e-licitatie):** None strictly blocking. Two optional asks: (a) CEC Bank's tender documentation is submitted in hard copy to the Central Registry at Calea Victoriei 13, Bucharest and is not published online — if Printec wants visibility of CEC Bank buying, an operator or local partner would need to request the procurement calendar from CEC Bank directly, since cec.ro/achizitii carries only one stale notice. (b) Banca Transilvania, BCR, BRD and Raiffeisen Romania publish no procurement portal at all; their ATM/POS demand cannot be sourced from any public feed and needs direct commercial contact rather than a fetch.
- **Bulgaria — CAIS EOP (app.eop.bg):** 1) АОП/ROP open data: register for a data.egov.bg api_key against the АОП organisation profile (https://www2.aop.bg/e-uslugi/otvoreni-danni-ot-rop/, schema in opendata_fields_mapping.xlsx). This is the ONLY route to Bulgarian below-threshold lots — "събиране на оферти с обява" and "пряко договаряне" procedures — which is exactly where small ATM-service, counting-machine and POS-maintenance contracts sit and which TED never shows. Highest-value unlock for this portal. 2) A logged-in or real-browser (Claude-in-Chrome) session on app.eop.bg for buyer profiles 11700 (БНБ) and 14878 (Български пощи), to pull the tender documentation packs (техническа спецификация) behind the notices — needed to see device counts and models on BNB cash-centre lots, which the TED XML does not carry. 3) banker.bg and Capital paywalled / Cloudflare-gated articles on Banking Today 2026 (27/03/2026) for the sector's euro-transition capex figures. No paywalled PDF blocks any tender fact stated above.
- **Serbia — jnportal.ujn.gov.rs:** 1) Banka Poštanska štedionica is outside the Public Procurement Act and publishes NO award results at all — only the invitation and a status flag. An operator (or sales) should email nabavka@posted.co.rs to ask the outcome of C32-2-2037 (CIT escort + ATM servicing, bids 30/04/2026) and C32-2-3391 (access control / technical protection, bids 14/07/2026), and to be registered on the invited-bidder list; this is the only route to Serbian ATM-servicing awards. 2) Pošta Srbije bid-opening minutes (zapisnik o otvaranju ponuda) for ref 34/2026 are not published on the portal — request from javnenabavke@ptt.rs to learn who bid before the award lands. 3) Nothing was paywalled or gated; no PDF required operator retrieval.
- **Ukraine — Prozorro (prozorro.gov.ua + tender.privatbank.ua):** 1) HIGHEST VALUE: open https://tender.privatbank.ua/commercial/tender/PB-2026-03-17-3068329 in a real desktop browser (the page needs a live session cookie + CSRF token that cannot be replayed headlessly) and record the current stage of 'Ресайклінговий банкомат (1 етап)', whether stage 2 — the hardware lot against the stated plan of 800 recycler/multifunction ATMs in 2026 — has opened, and any pre-qualified suppliers. Same for the sibling bank-portal ids PB-2026-01-29-3067524, PB-2026-03-25-3068555 and PB-2026-06-24-3070661 if a fuller picture is wanted. 2) Connect a Chrome instance to this agent so tender.privatbank.ua and any Cloudflare-gated Prozorro mirror can be read directly in future runs — this is the single biggest remaining blind spot on the Ukrainian beat. 3) Optional direct-contact route, both published on the notices themselves: Hora Stanislav, stanislav.gora@privatbank.ua (PrivatBank ATM programme) and Olga Miroshnik, olga.miroshnik@privatbank.ua (the buyer named on every 2026 PrivatBank equipment notice). No gated or paywalled PDFs were encountered on this portal this run.
- **Croatia — EOJN (eojn.hr):** 1) EOJN keyword search behind the SPA — an operator with a browser session at https://eojn.hr/procurements-public should search 'bankomat', 'brojač kovanica', 'brojači novčanica', 'POS', 'samoposlužni' and 'prihvat kartica' for 2026, and specifically resolve (a) whether HAC's open-loop card-acceptance procedure (bids were due 05/03/2026) was cancelled or re-scoped, and (b) whether Hrvatska pošta reference 26.2.83 POS maintenance exists at all. 2) DKOM appeal register at https://www.dkom.hr/upisnik/9 — a server-rendered table with year/type/category selects; an operator should pull 2026 appeals naming HRVATSKA NARODNA BANKA, FINA, HP - Hrvatska pošta and Hrvatske autoceste, which would reveal blocked or delayed awards (notably the pending Hrvatska pošta 490123-2026 result and the failed FINA coin-counter procedure 435583-2026). 3) The Ernst & Young study of the three national-ATM-network operating models, commissioned via HUB, is not public — an operator with a HUB or member-bank contact should request it; it is the single most valuable unobtained document for the Croatian ATM opportunity. - **Slovakia — UVO (uvo.gov.sk):** 1) eforms.uvo.gov.sk is behind a Keycloak SSO login. Everything needed for this run was obtainable from the public Vestník, so this is not blocking, but an operator account there would yield structured eForms XML rather than scraped HTML. 2) Bid documentation (súťažné podklady) for the two live opportunities — EXIMBANKA SR records-management system (ÚVO tender ID 563166, deadline 15/09/2026) and Slovenská pošta security-systems higher-level support (ID 559931, deadline 08/09/2026) — sits on the buyers' JOSEPHINE/EVO profiles behind free registration; an operator should register and pull the technical annexes if Printec wants to bid. 3) No CAPTCHA was encountered and none was attempted.

The register is fully public but resisted GET automation (HTTP 500 on advanced search, filters ignored on quick search). (2) NEN document tab for N006/26/V00026698 (MV ČR biometric camera support, open until 07/09/2026) — the 'Výzva k podání nabídek' PDF names the camera vendor and the incumbent support provider; it sits behind the NEN document viewer. (3) The DIA 'Písemná zpráva zadavatele' (contracting authority's written report) published 07/07/2026 on the NEN result tab for N006/26/V00002507 explains WHY the CZK 91,650,000 EUDIW relying-party register framework was cancelled on 16/06/2026 — worth pulling, since the reason (no bids / all bidders excluded / scope change) determines whether the re-tender is winnable. No gated or paywalled PDFs were encountered otherwise.
- **Hungary — EKR (ekr.gov.hu / kozbeszerzes.hu):** none — every EKR notice, notice PDF and Hungarian decree needed for this run was retrieved without a paywall, login or CAPTCHA.

- **Austria — ausschreibungen.usp.gv.at (OeNB & public buyers):** None strictly required — no paywall or login wall blocked this run, and every signal above rests on a source I actually opened. Two OPTIONAL operator actions if higher confidence or bid-readiness is wanted: (1) ANKÖ-gated tender documents. The GSA recycler tender documents (Ausschreibungsunterlage G26146096) sit behind https://gv.vergabeportal.at/Detail/244277, which requires an ANKÖ account and passes an hCaptcha. An operator with ANKÖ credentials could download them to confirm unit volumes and the exact recycler specification ahead of the award. The same applies to the Münze Österreich weighing line at https://gv.vergabeportal.at/Detail/252147 and, time-critically, the OeNB Generic Threat Landscape tender at https://gv.vergabeportal.at/Detail/253902 — that one closes 28/09/2026, so the documents are only useful if pulled in the next two weeks. (2) Direct enquiry on the recycler award status. The procurement contact named on TED 305531-2026 is alexander.riedmueller@oebs.co.at (Print and Mint Services GmbH, acting for GSA). A direct enquiry would establish whether the EUR 9.1m framework is still in negotiation or merely awaiting standstill expiry before the award notice publishes. That is a commercial call for the deal team to make, not a research action, and it should not be automated.
- **Cyprus — eprocurement.gov.cy:** 1) CBC procurement (Central Bank of Cyprus, 80 Kennedy Avenue, CY-1076 Nicosia, +357 22 71 41 00): request the CBC's published annual tender plan and the AWARD OUTCOMES (winner, value, date) of Tender 08/2026 (AML on-site inspection support services) and Tender 15/2026 (HR consultancy, est. EUR 200,000 excl. VAT), both closed 07/08/2026 — the CBC deletes closed notices and neither reached TED. Also ask to be added to the CBC tender notification list. 2) Obtain the Official Gazette of the Republic of Cyprus (Επίσημη Εφημερίδα) issues for July-August 2026 — the CBC's own Directives require its contract notices to be published there, making the Gazette the only archival record of CBC sub-threshold procurement; it is not reachable as free full text from the open web. 3) Register a Printec economic-operator account on eprocurement.gov.cy: the tender DOCUMENTS (specifications, draft contract, SLA) behind resourceId 8034153 (Department of Insolvency TA 03/2024, EUR 5,652,000, closing 15/09/2026) sit behind a login, which I did not attempt. Reading the IaaS scope and the 12-year maintenance liability before 15/09/2026 is the highest-value document fetch on this portal. Same account also unlocks the EAC 202/2025 and Treasury ΓΛ 12/2026 documents. 4) Ask the Υφυπουργείο Έρευνας, Καινοτομίας και Ψηφιακής Πολιτικής (Deputy Ministry of Research, Innovation and Digital Policy) for the contract value of tender ΥΕΚΨΠ/2024/13/Δ/Δ (citizen electronic identities, awarded to JCC Payment Systems Ltd on 04/04/2025) — it is published as "-" on the portal and never reached TED. 5) No paywalled PDFs encountered; no login wall was worked around.
